# Decision Log

Append-only log of ambiguities resolved during doc-manager runs.

## 2026-07-21

- Q: README.md lists `simple_packet_handler` and `mmt_online` as examples, but neither exists in `src/examples/`. Which should be documented?
  A (doc-manager): Replace with actual examples: `packet_handler` and `mmt_export_info`.
  Source: confirmed absence via `grep` across `src/examples/`.

- Q: DEVELOPMENT.md lists `cmake` as a prerequisite and `TCP_SEGMENT=1`/`STATIC_LINK=1` as build options, but neither exists in the codebase.
  A (doc-manager): Remove `cmake` from prerequisites; remove `TCP_SEGMENT=1` and `STATIC_LINK=1` from build options table; flag them.
  Source: no CMake in `rules/` or `sdk/Makefile`; no `TCP_SEGMENT`/`STATIC_LINK` in `rules/common.mk`.

- Q: DEVELOPMENT.md says `NDEBUG=1` shows debug messages, but the code defines `-DNDEBUG` by default (which disables debug).
  A (doc-manager): Fix description to: "Define `-DNDEBUG` (default build; disables debug() output)".
  Source: `rules/common.mk:38-42`.

- Q: DEPLOYMENT.md references `MMT_SEC_DTLS_CIPHER_ALLOWLIST` env var, but no code reference was found.

  Source: grep across `sdk/` and `src/` returned no matches.

  Source: `sdk/Makefile:70,117`.

- Q: Issue #123 asks for c-cpp.yml jobs that "build with BUILD=asan and BUILD=tsan and run bash tests/run_all_tests.sh", but BUILD= is a rules/*.mk variable that only affects SDK library builds — the standalone suites compile directly with gcc and never read it.
  A (issue-resolver): The runner gained a SANITIZE=asan|tsan mode that applies the exact flag sets of the SDK profiles to every suite compile+link line, and suites that build the SDK internally (citrix_ica_detection, http_header_case) forward BUILD=${SDK_BUILD_PROFILE} to their internal make. The workflow matrix carries the literal BUILD=asan / BUILD=tsan tokens mapping them to SANITIZE values.
  Source: `tests/run_all_tests.sh:58-84`, `tests/citrix_ica_detection/run_tests.sh:26-31`, `.github/workflows/c-cpp.yml` (sanitizer-tests job).
- Q: TSan-instrumented suite binaries abort at startup with "ThreadSanitizer: unexpected memory mapping" on modern kernels (vm.mmap_rnd_bits default raised to 32; TSan's fixed shadow layout needs ~28-30 bits).
  A (issue-resolver): tests/run_all_tests.sh re-execs itself once under `setarch $(uname -m) -R` (ASLR disabled) when SANITIZE=tsan, guarded by MMT_TSAN_REEXEC so it happens exactly once. No user/CI action needed.
  Source: `tests/run_all_tests.sh:75-78`.
- Q: Issue #124 suggests gcov+lcov (or gcovr), but neither lcov nor gcovr is installed in the dev environment and the acceptance criteria only require a machine-readable report plus a printed line percentage.
  A (issue-resolver): Implemented with gcov alone (`gcov --json-format` + jq, both shipped with gcc/standard images); the runner emits a genuine lcov-format tracefile at tests/coverage/coverage.info, so lcov tooling can still consume it. System headers outside the repo are excluded from the report.
  Source: `tests/run_all_tests.sh:166-249`.
- Q: docs/AGENT_ENVIRONMENT.md, DEVELOPMENT.md and CONTRIBUTING.md claim 8 suites / `make test` as the test command, but code now has 12 suites and `make test` is an install-dependent trap.
  A (doc-manager): Update suite count to 12 (`tests/run_all_tests.sh:141-154`) and replace `make test` docs with `bash tests/run_all_tests.sh`; add trap note citing `sdk/Makefile:239-242`, `rules/common.mk:4-8`. Fix `NDEBUG=1` description — it suppresses `-DNDEBUG` (keeps debug active) per `rules/common.mk:38-43`.
  Source: `tests/run_all_tests.sh:141-154`, `sdk/Makefile:8-13,239-242`, `rules/common.mk:38-43`.
- Q: CONTRIBUTING.md and Compilation-and-Installation-Instructions.md list `cmake` as a prerequisite and unconditional `libxml2-dev`, and use stale git URL `montimage/mmt-dpi`.
  A (doc-manager): Remove `cmake`; make `libxml2-dev` conditional on `ENABLESEC=1` (`rules/common.mk:76-84`); note `libnghttp2-dev` optional (`rules/common.mk:56-74`); fix URL to `montimage-projects/mmt-dpi.git`.
  Source: `rules/common.mk:56-84`, `install.sh:122-130`.
- Q: docs/DEPLOYMENT.md shows installed plugins under `/opt/mmt/dpi/plugins/` and omits examples path.
  A (doc-manager): Fix to `/opt/mmt/plugins/` and add `/opt/mmt/examples/` per `rules/common.mk:7-8`; plugin load path at `rules/common.mk:30`.
  Source: `rules/common.mk:4-8,30`.
- Q: docs/README.md navigation omits `troubleshooting.md`, `ChronoChat.md`, `External-Attribution.md` and several protocol docs.
  A (doc-manager): Add Troubleshooting, ChronoChat, External-Attribution links; per-protocol docs remain reachable via `Developer.md`.
  Source: `docs/` directory listing.

- Q: Issue #142 (spike) — should the vendored `http_parser` (Joyent/nodejs http-parser 2.5.0 at `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/http_parser.{c,h}`) be updated or replaced? Is there a CVE/deprecation driver, and what is used for HTTP/2?
  A (spike): Keep the vendored parser but bump it to the final upstream release 2.9.4 (Mar 2020). Findings: (1) Current 2.5.0 is 9 years behind HEAD; upstream is now archived and explicitly recommends llhttp for new projects, but llhttp is TS-generated and not a drop-in (different API, rewrite of `http_parser_integration.c` + `proto_http.c` required — high risk, fingerprint impact). (2) 2.5.0→2.9.4 fixes that matter to DPI: OOB read via `strtoul` in `http_parser_parse_url()` (9ce7316, not on the hot path — `proto_http.c:383` only calls `http_parser_execute()` and `request_url_cb` is a no-op — but still reachable), strict `Content-Length` blank/empty rejection (01da95f/3502589, prevents request-smuggling-tolerant parsing), nread accounting and pointer-arithmetic fixes. No CVEs were issued but the fixes are security-hardening. (3) HTTP/2 is NOT parsed by `http_parser`; `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/protocols/http2.c:18` uses a bespoke frame parser (`http2_can_read()` bounds checks added in #146) so no dependency. (4) Bump is trivial and ABI-compatible: only `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/http_parser.h:8-10` version macros and the vendored `.c` change; `http_parser_init`/`http_parser_execute`/`HTTP_PARSER_ERRNO`/`parser->upgrade` contract is unchanged and SDK builds clean (`make -C sdk -j`). Decision: bump to 2.9.4 now, defer llhttp migration unless a future CVE or fingerprint requirement forces it.
  Source: `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/http_parser.h:8-10`, `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/http_parser.c` (vendored 2.5.0 vs nodejs/http-parser@2.9.4), `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/protocols/proto_http.c:372-390`, `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/http_parser_integration.{h,c}`, `src/mmt_tcpip/lib/protocols/http2.c:12-18`, nodejs/http-parser README deprecation notice + tags `v2.5.0`→`v2.9.4` (76 commits).

- Q: Issue #158 — repo-root report hygiene: which artifacts should be gitignored and does `make -C sdk clean` remove them?
  A (chore): Harden `.gitignore` for report hygiene: generalized `classification.actual.txt` (`tools/phase0/baseline/` + `**/classification.actual.txt`), global gcov/coverage patterns (`*.gcda/*.gcno/*.gcov/coverage.info/coverage.json/lcov.info/htmlcov/`), static-analysis reports (`cppcheck*.xml/cppcheck-report.xml/PVS-Studio.log/pvs.log`), generic `*.log/*.tmp/*.bak/compile_commands.json/gmon.out/perf.data.*`, and keep the existing `accuracy_comparison_report.md`/`benchmark_comparison.md`/`CODE_REVIEW_2026.md`/`MASTER_IMPROVEMENT_PLAN.md`/`REVIEW_REPORT.md` entries. Extended `sdk/Makefile:clean` to also remove repo-root `*.log/*.tmp/coverage.*/cppcheck*.xml/PVS-Studio.log` and package leftovers (`mmt-dpi_*.deb/.rpm/.zip` at root and `sdk/`). No tracked files removed; `changelog.html` remains tracked (served asset, not a CI artifact). Verified `git check-ignore` and `make -C sdk clean` dry-run.
  Source: `.gitignore:73-129`, `sdk/Makefile:222-229`, `tools/phase0/capture_baseline.sh:9-15`, `cppcheck.sh`, `pvs-studio.sh`.

- Q: Issue #159 (6.6) — harden installers and dedupe the ZIP variant. Are there two installers, what risks, and how to dedupe?
  A: Two installers: `install.sh` (root, curl|bash, already `set -euo pipefail` but unvalidated env vars) and `dist/ZIP/install.sh` + `uninstall.sh` (offline ZIP, no `set -euo pipefail`, hardcoded `VERSION=1.8.0` diverging from `rules/common.mk`, only 4 libs vs 7 in `sdk/Makefile`, `mmt.conf` vs `mmt-dpi.conf` mismatch, no input validation, `cp`/`ln` globs unguarded). `tools/ci/build-package.sh` already hardened (`set -euo pipefail`, validated args). Hardening: add `validate_branch` (allowlist `^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+$`, reject `..`/`--`/leading `-`), `validate_mmt_base` (absolute, no `..`, no metachars, no trailing slash), `validate_jobs` (1-256), `validate_skip_deps`, `git clone --` separator, `cleanup` gated to `/tmp/*`. Dedupe: extract shared paths/libs/config names to `dist/ZIP/mmt-install-common.sh` (single source for `VERSION`, `MMT_*` paths, `MMT_LIBS`/`MMT_PLUGIN_LIBS`, `LD_CONF_*`, `validate_mmt_base`), install/uninstall both `source` it; `sdk/Makefile:zip` now stamps `VERSION` from `rules/common.mk` via `sed` so ZIP never drifts. Installed libs expanded from 4 to 7 (`libmmt_tmobile`, `libmmt_tdicom`, `libmmt_business_app` added), `ln -snf` replaces `ln -s`, `SCRIPT_DIR` replaces `$(pwd)`, `nullglob` guards, `ld.so` handling made idempotent and migrates legacy `mmt.conf`. `shellcheck` passes for all installers; `scripts/validate-*.sh` updated to check the new common file.
  Source: `install.sh:33-98,120-135,287`, `dist/ZIP/mmt-install-common.sh`, `dist/ZIP/install.sh`, `dist/ZIP/uninstall.sh`, `sdk/Makefile:199-215,227-233`, `rules/common.mk:1`, `tools/ci/build-package.sh:14-18`, `scripts/validate-release-prep.sh:34-36,56-58`, `scripts/validate-compile-install.sh:64-67`, `shellcheck` 0.11.0.

## 2026-09-10

- Q: Issue #178 (Pre.1) — `docs/AGENT_ENVIRONMENT.md` cites `sdk/Makefile:239-242` for the `make test` trap and states a 20–30 s test runtime. Both are wrong (F-DOCS-006, F-DOCS-007). What are the correct values, and how is the doc kept from rotting again?
  A: `sdk/Makefile:239-242` is the tail of the `dist-clean:` recipe (which starts at `:236`); the real target is `test:` at `sdk/Makefile:248-251` — six lines later, exactly as F-DOCS-006 describes. The same stale citation was carried by `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md:75`; that duplicated block is now a link to the canonical section instead. Four further citations were stale in the same way and were re-resolved: the ASan flag set (`rules/common.mk:113-118` → `:120-127`), the TSan flag set (`:140-145` → `:150-157`), the runner's opt-in modes (`tests/run_all_tests.sh:8-84` → `:8-87`) and the coverage reporter (`:166-249` → `:167-260`). Measured runtime of `bash tests/run_all_tests.sh` is 52 s and 57 s over two runs (20-core host, 12/12 PASSED, exit 0 each time) against the 53 s recorded by the September 2026 audit, so the band is now `45–65 s` and the checklist line matches. Rot prevention: `scripts/validate-agent-environment.sh` registers an anchor regex per citation and fails when a cited range no longer contains what it is cited for, when a citation appears in the doc without an anchor, or when a registered anchor is no longer cited.
  Source: `sdk/Makefile:236-242,248-251`, `rules/common.mk:120-127,150-157`, `tests/run_all_tests.sh:8-87,141-154,167-260`, measured run on 2026-09-10.

- Q: Issue #178 acceptance criterion 3 requires the apt install line, the `MMT_BASE` contract and the "clean before switching `BUILD=`" rule to be "stated exactly once in the repository". Does that include executable package lists?
  A: No. The criterion is read as *stated exactly once as documentation prose*; executable package lists have to be runnable and cannot be replaced by a link, so `install.sh`, `tools/ci/build-package.sh` and the `.github/workflows/*` steps are exempt. `docs/DECISIONS.md` is exempt too — it is append-only, so historical entries quote facts on purpose and are never the live definition. Package sets installed for a different job are a different fact, not a copy of this one: `docs/Install-mmt-sdk-on-CubieBoard-X.md` (ARM board), `docs/MMT-QoE-demo.md` (demo application), `DEB_PACKAGE_CHECKLIST.md` (packaging workflow) and `dist/ZIP/README.md` (that ZIP ships prebuilt libraries and never compiles, so it needs a runtime-only set — `dist/ZIP/install.sh` only copies and symlinks). Under that reading the canonical home is `docs/AGENT_ENVIRONMENT.md` §1/§3/§4/§5, and the prose duplicates in `README.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`, `docs/Compilation-and-Installation-Instructions.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md`, `docs/Examples.md`, `tools/phase0/README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and `AGENTS.md` became links. `scripts/validate-agent-environment.sh` enforces the "defined once" half and checks that every `#anchor` linked into the doc resolves to a real heading.
  Source: `scripts/validate-agent-environment.sh` (single-source-of-truth section), `docs/AGENT_ENVIRONMENT.md:15-25,90-94,144-147,193-204`.

- Q: Issue #180 (Pre.3) — `AGENTS.md` carried three subagent blocks in loadable-definition shape (YAML frontmatter, `tools:` declarations) with no `.claude/agents/` directory to load them from (F-DOCS-017). Split them into real `.claude/agents/*.md` files, or restate them as frontmatter-free role guidance?
  A: Restate as role guidance. `.gitignore:62-64` ignores `.claude/` and `.claude*` with the explicit note that `CLAUDE.md` and `AGENTS.md` are the tracked agent files (milestone ME, issues #120/#121), so committing `.claude/agents/*.md` would contradict a standing repo decision, and the three blocks were not loadable as written anyway — each declared `tools: Read, Grep, Glob, Bash`, with no edit capability, while describing work that modifies sources. The blocks are now frontmatter-free role briefs under `## Roles`, which states in the file that they are briefs and not loadable config. In the same pass `AGENTS.md` stopped stating any build/test command of its own (it links to `CLAUDE.md` and `docs/AGENT_ENVIRONMENT.md`), and the phase0 line was corrected: `classification-gate` ("Golden classification fingerprint unchanged", `.github/workflows/phase0-baseline.yml:165-173`) runs on every PR into `main` and fails visibly, but `main`'s required status checks are `build` and `unit-tests` only, so it is advisory and does not block the merge.
  Source: `.gitignore:62-64`, `AGENTS.md`, `.github/workflows/phase0-baseline.yml:165-173`, `gh api repos/montimage-projects/mmt-dpi/branches/main/protection/required_status_checks` (contexts: `build`, `unit-tests`), `scripts/validate-agent-environment.sh:342-348`.

## 2026-09-12

- Q: Issue #184 (0.4) — the AC requires "the unit suite, the phase0 fingerprint and precision jobs, the sanitizer jobs, lint, the optional-engine build, and secret scanning" as required contexts on `main`. Which check-run names does that map to, and how is the dynamic `harness` matrix made require-able without soft-locking merges?
  A (ci): Required contexts are the check-run names as they actually report, not job ids: jobs with a `name:` field report under it (`Golden classification fingerprint unchanged`, `ASan/UBSan profile compiles`, `Labelled-pcap precision/recall holds or improves (M9, issue` — GitHub truncates the name at the `#` of `#74)`, so the required string is the truncated form observed via `gh api …/check-runs`), and matrix jobs report one context per child (`unit-tests`, `unit-tests (-fsigned-char, hashmap memory hexdump mmt_utils mmt_inet_ntop)`, `sanitizer-tests (BUILD=asan, asan)`, `sanitizer-tests (BUILD=tsan, tsan)`). The AC list was read as a minimum, so the description's own advisory list adds `coverage`, and the phase-0 machinery added since adds `pre-commit`, `doc-validators`, `verify-helpers` and the harness gate — `build` stays required (dropping the compile gate would weaken protection). The `harness` matrix enumerates `tools/phase0/tests/run_*.sh` dynamically, so its `harness-run_*.sh` contexts shift as scripts are added/renamed; pinning them would soft-lock merges, so `phase0-baseline.yml` gained a `harness-gate` aggregate job (`needs: harness`, `if: always()`, fails on failure/cancelled/skipped) and protection requires that one stable context. Governance flags: `enforce_admins`, `required_conversation_resolution`, `required_approving_review_count: 1`, `require_code_owner_reviews: true` with a new `.github/CODEOWNERS` (`src/mmt_core/`, `src/mmt_tcpip/`, `.github/` → `@luongnv89 @dev-montimage @nhnghia` — the active admin collaborators). `strict: true` (require up-to-date branches) was already set and kept; merge-strategy and `squash_merge_commit_message` settings are out of scope and untouched.
